} finally { Arrays.fill(probe, '\\0'); }\n    }\n\n    static byte[] shadowSalt(String username) {\n        try {\n            MessageDigest md = MessageDigest.getInstance(\"SHA-256\");\n            byte[] d = md.digest((\"portal-shadow:\" + username).getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            return Arrays.copyOf(d, SALT_BYTES);\n        } catch (GeneralSecurityException e) { throw new IllegalStateException(e); }\n    }\n\n    public static boolean matches(byte[] expected, byte[] actual) {\n        return MessageDigest.isEqual(expected, actual);\n    }\n    private static final char[] PROBE_PASSWORD = \"shadow-probe-password-0000\".toCharArray();\n}\n```\nNote `SecretKeySpec` not used — good, memory says key.destroy() throws. PROMPT_USER : PROMPT_CMD);\n            if (line == null) {\n                out.println(\"the portal is closing.\");\n                return EXIT_OK;\n            }\n            if (session == null) {\n                authenticateLine(line);\n                continue;\n            }\n            String command = line.strip().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);\n            if (command.isEmpty()) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            if (command.equals(\"quit\") || command.equals(\"exit\")) {\n                closeSession(\"ending the session.\");\n                out.println(\"the portal is closing.\");\n                return EXIT_OK;\n            }\n            if (command.equals(\"logout\")) {\n                closeSession(\"signed out.\");\n                continue;\n            }\n            execute(command);\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void authenticateLine(String username) throws IOException {\n        char[] password = askSecret(PROMPT_PASS);\n        LoginService.AuthResult result = login.verify(username, password);\n        out.println(result.message());\n        if (!result.granted()) {\n            out.println(\"access denied.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!record(\"SESSION_OPEN\", result.username(), \"role=\" + result.role())) {\n            out.println(\"access refused: the session could not be recorded.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        session = login.issueSession(result.username(), result.role());\n        out.println(\"signed in as \" + result.username() + \" (\" + result.role() + \").\");\n        out.println(\"type a command, or 'help' to see them.\");\n    }\n\n    private void execute(String command) throws IOException {\n        String[] parts = command.split(\"\\\\s+\", 2);\n        String verb = parts[0];\n        String argument = parts.length > 1 ? parts[1].strip() : \"\";\n        switch (verb) {\n            case \"help\" -> out.print(helpText());\n            case \"whoami\" -> whoami();\n            case \"status\" -> status();\n            case \"admins\" -> listAdmins();\n            case \"create\" -> create(argument);\n            case \"reset\" -> reset(argument);\n            case \"remove\" -> remove(argument);\n            case \"unlock\" -> unlock(argument);\n            case \"audit\" -> showAudit(argument);\n            default -> out.println(\"unknown command: \" + AuditLog.clean(verb) + \" (try 'help')\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void whoami() throws IOException {\n        if (!requireSession(\"whoami\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        out.println(\"you are \" + session.username() + \" with role \" + session.role()\n                + \", this session ends at \" + Instant.ofEpochMilli(session.expiresAtMillis()) + \".\");\n    }\n\n    private void status() throws IOException {\n        if (!requireSession(\"status\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        out.println(\"administrators on file : \" + store.size());\n        out.println(\"rows it could not read : \" + store.malformedRows());\n        out.println(\"credential file        : \" + store.path());\n        out.println(\"audit log              : \" + audit.path());\n        out.println(\"failed attempts        : \" + login.maxFailures() + \" before an account locks\");\n    }\n\n    private void listAdmins() throws IOException {\n        if (!mayManage(\"admins\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        List<String> names = new ArrayList<>(store.usernames());\n        if (names.isEmpty()) {\n            out.println(\"no accounts.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        for (String name : names) {\n            out.println(\"  \" + name + \"  \" + store.roleOf(name)\n                    + (login.isLockedOut(name) ? ask(PROMPT_USER) : argument;\n        String name = validName(raw);\n        if (name == null) {\n            return;\n        }\n        if (!store.contains(name)) {\n            out.println(\"there is no administrator called \" + AuditLog.clean(name) + \".\");\n            return;\n        }\n        char[] password = readAcceptedPassword(name);\n        if (password == null) {\n            out.println(\"the password was not changed.\");\n            return;\n        }\n        try {\n            if (!record(\"ADMIN_RESET\", session.username(), \"reset=\" + name)) {\n                return;\n            }\n            store.put(name, password, store.roleOf(name));\n            login.clearLock(name);\n            out.println(\"the password for \" + name + \" was replaced and its lock cleared.\");\n        } catch (GeneralSecurityException | IOException failure) {\n            out.println(\"the new password could not be saved: \" + safeMessage(failure));\n        } finally {\n            Arrays.fill(password, '\\0');\n        }\n    }\n\n    private void remove(String argument) throws IOException {\n        if (!mayManage(\"remove\")) {\n            return;\n        }\n        String raw = argument.isEmpty() ? Returns\n     * {@code null} when the operator gave up or every attempt was rejected.\n     */\n    private char[] readAcceptedPassword(String username) {\n        for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 3; loaded.malformedRows());\n\n        Path notAFile = scratch.resolve(\"store-is-a-directory\");\n        Files.createDirectories(notAFile);\n        boolean threw = false;\n        try {\n            new CredentialStore(notAFile);\n        } catch (IOException expected) {\n            threw = true;\n        }\n        ok(\"a directory where the store belongs is an error\", threw, \"directory accepted as store\");\n\n        Path storeForTamper = scratch.resolve(\"store-tampered.tsv\");\n        CredentialStore live = new CredentialStore(storeForTamper);\n        live.put(\"carol\", \"Carol-Passw0rd-3\".toCharArray(), Roles.SUPERVISOR);\n        CredentialStore.AdminCredential row = live.find(\"carol\");\n        byte[] weakSalt = Arrays.copyOf(row.salt(), 8);\n        Files.writeString(storeForTamper, \"carol\\t100000\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64()\n                + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore tampered = new CredentialStore(storeForTamper);\n        ok(\"a row whose key was replaced by junk is refused\", tampered.size() == 0,\n                \"junk key accepted\");\n        ok(\"the salt column of a good row keeps its length\", row.salt().length == Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES,\n                \"salt \" + weakSalt.length);\n    }\n\n    // ----------------------------------------------------------- authenticate\n\n    private static void authentication() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"auth\", 40);\n        LoginService.AuthResult good = f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password is granted\", good.status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                String.valueOf(good.status()));\n        ok(\"the granted result carries the stored role\", Roles.SUPERVISOR.equals(good.role()),\n                String.valueOf(good.role()));\n        ok(\"the password array is wiped after a grant\", isAllNul(goodPasswordHolder()),\n                \"characters left in the array\");\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult wrongCase = f.service.verify(\"ALICE\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the username is matched case-insensitively\",\n                wrongCase.status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED, String.valueOf(wrongCase.status()));\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult wrong = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the wrong password is denied\", wrong.status() == LoginService.Status.DENIED,\n                String.valueOf(wrong.status()));\n        ok(\"a denial gives away nothing\", \"the username or password is not correct\"\n                .equals(wrong.message()), wrong.message());\n        ok(\"the password array is wiped after a denial\", isAllNul(\"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray()),\n                \"caller array untouched\");\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult ghost = f.service.verify(\"nobody\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"an unknown username is denied\", ghost.status() == LoginService.Status.DENIED,\n                String.valueOf(ghost.status()));\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult blank = f.service.verify(\"alice\", new char[0]);\n        ok(\"an empty password is rejected before any work\", blank.elapsedNanos() < 5_000_000L\n                && blank.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, blank.status() + \" \"\n                + blank.elapsedNanos());\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult nul = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                (\"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NUL).toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password plus a trailing null is refused\",\n                nul.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, String.valueOf(nul.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult tabbed = f.service.verify(\"alice\" + TAB,\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password for a tab-padded name is refused\",\n                tabbed.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, String.valueOf(tabbed.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult surrogate = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                (\"Alice-Passw0rd-\" + (char) 0xD842).toCharArray());\n        ok(\"an unpaired surrogate password is refused\",\n                surrogate.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED,\n                String.valueOf(surrogate.status()));\n\n        Fixture g = fixture(\"auth-unhashable\", 40);\n        Path storePath = g.store.path();\n        CredentialStore.AdminCredential aliceRow = g.store.find(\"alice\");\n        Files.writeString(storePath, \"alice\\t100000\\t\" + shortSaltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64()\n                + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore reload = new CredentialStore(storePath);\n        ok(\"a row with a short salt is never loaded\", reload.size() == 0, \"size \" + reload.size());\n        ok(\"so alice is simply unknown\", reload.find(\"alice\") == null, \"row still present\");\n        ok(\"and the good row of the fixture was really the one replaced\",\n                aliceRow.salt().length == Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES, \"fixture salt already short\");\n    }\n\n    private static char[] goodPasswordHolder() {\n        return \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray();\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isAllNul(char[] value) {\n        char[] copy = value.clone();\n        Arrays.fill(copy, '\\0');\n        for (char c : copy) {\n            if (c != '\\0') {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------- lockout per name\n\n    private static void lockoutIsPerAccount() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"lockout\", 40);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        }\n        ok(\"three failures leave alice usable\", f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray()).status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                \"locked too early\");\n\n        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            LoginService.AuthResult r = f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n            if (i == 2) {\n                ok(\"the attempt that reaches the limit says locked\",\n                        r.status() == LoginService.Status.LOCKED, String.valueOf(r.status()));\n            }\n        }\n        LoginService.AuthResult whileLocked = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password is refused while locked\",\n                whileLocked.status() == LoginService.Status.LOCKED,\n                String.valueOf(whileLocked.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult otherName = f.service.verify(\"bob\",\n                \"Bob-Passw0rd-22\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"another administrator is not locked out\",\n                otherName.status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED, String.valueOf(otherName.status()));\n        f.service.clearLock(\"alice\");\n        ok(\"a supervisor unlock restores access\", f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray()).status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                \"unlock did not help\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------- equal cost\n\n    private static void equalCostBranches() throws Exception {\n        Fixture plain = fixture(\"cost\", 400);\n        Fixture locked = fixture(\"cost-lock\", 2, 60L * 60L * 1000L * 24L);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            locked.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        }\n        ok(\"the cost fixture account is locked for the timing run\",\n                locked.service.isLockedOut(\"alice\"), \"not locked\");\n\n        Fixture broken = fixture(\"cost-broken\", 400);\n        Files.writeString(broken.store.path(), \"alice\\t100000\\t\" + shortSaltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64()\n                + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore brokenReload = new CredentialStore(broken.store.path());\n        ok(\"the unhashable branch is really an unknown account\",\n                brokenReload.find(\"alice\") == null, \"row loaded anyway\");\n\n        List<Long> knownBad = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> unknown = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> isLocked = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> badName = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> blankish = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (int round = 0; round < 3; round++) {\n            knownBad.add(plain.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            unknown.add(plain.service.verify(\"nobody-here\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            isLocked.add(locked.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            badName.add(plain.service.verify(\"x\" + TAB, \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            blankish.add(plain.service.verify(\"alice\", (\"p\" + NUL).toCharArray()).elapsedNanos());\n        }\n        long realWork = median(knownBad);\n        ok(\"a real derivation is measured, not a rounding artefact\", realWork > 20_000_000L,\n                \"median \" + ms(realWork) + \" ms\");\n        ok(\"a wrong password on a known account is refused\", median(knownBad) > 0, \"no sample\");\n        ok(\"an unknown username costs the same as a known one\",\n                withinFactor(unknown, knownBad, 2.0), ratio(unknown, knownBad) + \" (\"\n                        + ms(median(unknown)) + \" vs \" + ms(median(knownBad)) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"an attempt while locked costs the same\", withinFactor(isLocked, knownBad, 2.0),\n                ratio(isLocked, knownBad) + \" (\" + ms(median(isLocked)) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"a malformed username costs the same as a real check\",\n                withinFactor(badName, knownBad, 2.0),\n                ratio(badName, knownBad) + \" (\" + ms(median(badName)) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"the four timed branches all stay inside one band\",\n                spread(knownBad, unknown, isLocked, badName) <= 2.0,\n                \"spread \" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"%.2f\",\n                        spread(knownBad, unknown, isLocked, badName)) + \"x\");\n        ok(\"an unusable password is refused cheaply\", median(blankish) < realWork / 10,\n                ms(median(blankish)) + \" ms against \" + ms(realWork) + \" ms\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------ audit trail\n\n    private static void auditTrail() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"audit\", 40);\n        f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        f.service.verify(\"nobody\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        List<String> lines = f.audit.tail(100);\n        ok(\"every attempt is written down\", lines.size() == 3, \"records \" + lines.size());\n        ok(\"grants and denials are labelled\", lines.get(0).contains(\"AUTH_GRANTED\")\n                && lines.get(1).contains(\"AUTH_DENIED\") && lines.get(2).contains(\"AUTH_DENIED\")),\n        ok(\"records are numbered in order\", lines.get(0).startsWith(\"1\" + TAB)\n                && lines.get(2).startsWith(\"3\" + TAB), \"sequence \" + lines.get(2));\n        ok(\"no password reaches the log\", !Files.readString(f.audit.path()).contains(\"Alice-Passw0rd\"),\n                \"password found in the log\");\n        ok(\"a reopened log continues the numbering\",\n                new AuditLog(f.audit.path()).tail(100).get(2).startsWith(\"3\" + TAB), \"numbering reset\");\n    }\n\n    private static void auditTrailCannotBeForged() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"audit-sanitise\", 40);\n        f.audit.record(\"EVA\", \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"9999\" + TAB + \"FORGED\", \"detail\");\n        List<String> lines = f.audit.tail(10);\n        ok(\"hostile input stays inside one record\", lines.size() == 1, \"records \" + lines.size());\n        ok(\"newlines and tabs are replaced\", lines.get(0).contains(\"alice?9999?FORGED\"),\n                lines.get(0));\n        ok(\"a record keeps exactly its five fields\",\n                lines.get(0).chars().filter(c -> c == TAB).count() == 4, \"field count off\");\n        f.audit.record(\"EVB\", \"x\".repeat(300), \"d\");\n        List<String> after = f.audit.tail(10);\n        ok(\"an over-long field is truncated\",\n                after.get(1).split(TAB + \"\", -1)[3].length() == AuditLog.MAX_FIELD_CHARS,\n                \"field not truncated\");\n        ok(\"an empty field is written as a dash\", \"-\".equals(AuditLog.clean(\"\")),\n                AuditLog.clean(\"\"));\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------- fail closed\n\n    private static void failClosedWhenUnrecordable() throws Exception {\n        Path control = scratch.resolve(\"audit-control.log\");\n        AuditLog good = new AuditLog(control);\n        good.record(\"CTRL\", \"alice\", \"one\");\n        good.record(\"CTRL\", \"alice\", \"two\");\n        ok(\"the control log really accepts records\", Files.readString(control).lines().count() == 2,\n                \"control log empty\");\n\n        Path blocked = scratch.resolve(\"audit-blocked.log\");\n        Files.createDirectories(blocked);\n        Fixture f = new Fixture(new sessionLifecycle() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"session\", 40);\n        LoginService.AuthResult granted = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        LoginService.Session live = f.service.issueSession(granted.username(), granted.role());\n        ok(\"a fresh session authorises admin commands\", f.service.authorized(live),\n                \"fresh session refused\");\n        LoginService.Session expired = new LoginService.Session(live.token(), live.username(),\n                live.role(), System.currentTimeMillis() - 1);\n        ok(\"an expired session does not\", !f.service.authorized(expired), \"expired session kept\");\n        ok(\"a session with no token does not\",\n                !f.service.authorized(new LoginService.Session(null, \"alice\", Roles.SUPERVISOR,\n                        System.currentTimeMillis() + 60_000)), \"tokenless session kept\");\n        ok(\"a 60_000)), \"unknown role kept\");\n        LoginService.Session second = f.service.issueSession(granted.username(), granted.role());\n        ok(\"sessions get a different token each time\", !live.token().equals(second.token()),\n                \"token reused\");\n        ok(\"only a supervisor may manage accounts\", Roles.mayManageAccounts(Roles.SUPERVISOR)\n                && !Roles.mayManageAccounts(Roles.OPERATOR), \"role table wrong\");\n        ok(\"an auditor may still read the trail\", Roles.mayReadAudit(Roles.AUDITOR),\n                \"auditor refused\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------- real child process\n\n    private static void commandLine() throws Exception {\n        Path home = password: \") == 1,\n                \"new=\" + count(out, \"new password: \") + \" repeat=\" + count(out, \"repeat password: \"));\n        ok(\"the weak password names its own problem\", scratch.resolve(\"cli-roles\");\n        runCli(second, \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE + \"create carol\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"Carol-Passw0rd-5\" + NEWLINE + \"Carol-Passw0rd-5\" + NEWLINE + \"quit\" + NEWLINE, null);\n        String asOperator = runCli(second, \"carol\" + NEWLINE + \"Carol-Passw0rd-5\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"create dave\" + NEWLINE + \"audit 5\" + NEWLINE + \"quit\" + NEWLINE, null);\n        ok(\"an operator cannot NEWLINE\n                + \"Nope-Passw0rd-9\" + NEWLINE, null));\n        ok(\"a wrong password and an unknown username answer identically\",\n                wrongPassword.equals(unknownUser) && wrongPassword.startsWith(\"the username or\"),\n                quoted(wrongPassword) + \" against \" + quoted(unknownUser));\n        ok(\"the denial text is the generic one\",\n                \"the noLog.contains(\"access denied.\") && !noLog.contains(\"signed in as alice\"),\n                tail(noLog));\n        ok(\"and it says the login could not be recorded\",\n                noLog.contains(\"access refused: the login could not be recorded.\"), tail(noLog));\n\n        Path locked = scratch.resolve(\"cli-empty-abort\");\n        String aborted = runCli(locked, \"al\" + NEWLINE + NEWLINE, null);\n        ok(\"an unusable bootstrap keeps the portal locked\",\n                aborted.contains(\"the portal stays locked.\"), tail(aborted));\n\n        Path self = scratch.resolve(\"cli-selftest\");\n        String report = runCli(self, \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE, \"self-test\");\n        ok(\"self-test refuses all three probes\",\n                report.contains(\"-> DENIED\") && report.contains(\"blank password   -> REJECTED\"),\n                tail(report));\n        ok(\"self-test prints a measurable cost per probe\",\n                report.contains(\"ms\") && report.contains(\"wrong password   -> DENIED in \"),\n                tail(report));\n    }\n\n    /** Runs the portal in a child JVM with piped stdin, returning its whole output. */\n    private static String runCli(Path home, String stdin, String... args) throws Exception {\n        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of(System.getProperty(\"java.home\")\n                + File.separator + \"bin\" + File.separator + \"java\", \"-cp\",\n                new File(System.getProperty(\"java.class.path\")).getAbsolutePath(),\n                \"-Dportal.home=\" + home.toAbsolutePath(), \"portal.AdminPortal\"));\n        command.addAll(Arrays.asList(args));\n        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(command);\n        builder.redirectErrorStream(true);\n        Process child = builder.start();\n        try (OutputStream stdinStream = child.getOutputStream()) {\n            stdinStream.write(stdin.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            stdinStream.flush();\n        }\n        String output;\n        try (InputStream in = child.getInputStream()) {\n            output = new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        }\n        int code = child.waitFor();\n        if (code != 0 && code != 2) {\n            fail(\"the portal child exited with \" + code + \": \" + tail(output));\n        }\n        return output;\n    }\n\n    /** Runs an in-process portal with piped input, returning its whole output. */\n    private static String runPortal(Fixture f, String stdin, boolean unused) {\n        ByteArrayOutputStream captured = new ByteArrayOutputStream();\n        AdminPortal portal = new AdminPortal(f.store, f.audit, f.service,\n                new java.io.StringReader(stdin), new java.io.PrintStream(captured, true,\n                        StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n        try {\n            portal.run(new String[0]);\n        } catch (IOException failure) {\n            return captured + \" (io: \" + failure + \")\";\n        }\n        return captured.toString(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ plumbing\n\n    private record Fixture(CredentialStore store, AuditLog audit, LoginService service) {\n    }\n\n    private static Fixture fixture(String name, int maxFailures) throws IOException {\n        return fixture(name, maxFailures, LoginService.DEFAULT_LOCKOUT_MILLIS);\n    }\n\n    private static Fixture fixture(String name, int maxFailures, long lockoutMillis)\n            throws IOException {\n        Path dir = scratch.resolve(name);\n        Files.createDirectories(dir);\n        CredentialStore store = new CredentialStore(dir.resolve(\"credentials.tsv\"));\n        store.put(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray(), Roles.SUPERVISOR);\n        store.put(\"bob\", \"Bob-Passw0rd-22\".toCharArray(), Roles.OPERATOR);\n        AuditLog audit = new AuditLog(dir.resolve(\"audit.log\"));\n        LoginService service = new LoginService(store, audit, maxFailures, lockoutMillis,\n                LoginService.DEFAULT_SESSION_TTL_MILLIS);\n        return new Fixture(store, audit, service);\n    }\n\n    private static String saltB64() {\n        byte[] salt = new byte[Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES];\n        for (int i = 0; i < salt.length; i++) {\n            salt[i] = (byte) (i + 1);\n        }\n        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(salt);\n    }\n\n    private static String shortSaltB64() {\n        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(new byte[8]);\n    }\n\n    private static String keyB64() {\n        byte[] key = new byte[Pbkdf2.KEY_BYTES];\n        for (int i = 0; i < key.length; i++) {\n            key[i] = (byte) (200 - i);\n        }\n        return java.util.Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(key);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean ownerOnly(Path file) throws IOException {\n        return Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file).equals(java.util.EnumSet.of(\n                java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermission.OWNER_READ,\n                java.nio.file.attribute.PosixFilePermission.OWNER_WRITE));\n    }\n\n    private static boolean throwsIae(Runnable body) {\n        try {\n            body.run();\n            return false;\n        } catch (IllegalArgumentException expected) {\n            return true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    private static long median(List<Long> samples) {\n        List<Long> sorted = new ArrayList<>(samples);\n        sorted.sort(Long::compare);\n        return sorted.get(sorted.size() / 2);\n    }\n\n    private static boolean withinFactor(List<Long> sample, List<Long> reference, double factor) {\n        double ratio = (double) median(sample) / (double) median(reference);\n        return ratio >= 1.0 / factor && ratio <= factor;\n    }\n\n    private static String ratio(List<Long> sample, List<Long> reference) {\n        return String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"%.2fx\",\n                (double) median(sample) / (double) median(reference));\n    }\n\n    private static double spread(List<Long>... rejected(\"Abcd-1234!\", \"alice\"));\n        ok(\"a password containing the username is rejected\",\n                rejected(\"Alice-Tester-99\", \"alice\") != null, \"username leak accepted\");\n        ok(\"a password without a digit is rejected\", rejected(\"abcdefghij!\", \"alice\") != null,\n                \"letter-only accepted\");\n        ok(\"a password without a letter is rejected\", (char) 0xDE42, \"alice\") == null,\n                rejected(\"Abcd-1234!\" + (char) 0xD842 + (char) 0xDE42, \"alice\"));\n        ok(\"an oversized password is refused\",\n                rejected(\"Abcd-1234!\" + \"x\".repeat(PasswordPolicy.MAX_CHARS), \"alice\") != null,\n                \"oversized accepted\");\n        ok(\"a null password is refused\", PasswordPolicy.structural(null) != null, \"null accepted\");\n        ok(\"an empty password is refused\", PasswordPolicy.structural(new char[0]) != null,\n                \"empty accepted\");\n        ok(\"a full password of 1024 characters is accepted\",\n                rejected(\"Abcd-1234!\" + \"y\".repeat(PasswordPolicy.MAX_CHARS - 10), \"alice\") == null,\n                rejected(\"Abcd-1234!\" + \"y\".repeat(PasswordPolicy.MAX_CHARS - 10), \"alice\"));\n    }\n\n    private static String rejected(String password, String username) {\n        return PasswordPolicy.check(password.toCharArray(), username);\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------- usernames\n\n    private static void usernameRules() {\n        ok(\"names are lower-cased\", \"alice\".equals(Names.normalize(\"Alice\")),\n                Names.normalize(\"Alice\"));\n        ok(\"a leading space is refused, not trimmed\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\" alice\")),\n                \"space-padded name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a trailing tab is refused, not trimmed\",\n                throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"alice\" + TAB)), \"tab-padded name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a trailing newline is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"alice\" + NEWLINE)),\n                \"newline-padded name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a trailing null is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"alice\" + NUL)),\n                \"NUL-padded name accepted\");\n        ok(\"an embedded tab is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"al\" + TAB + \"ce\")),\n                \"tab inside name accepted\");\n        ok(\"an embedded space is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"ali ce\")),\n                \"space inside name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a two character name is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"al\")),\n                \"two character name accepted\");\n        ok(\"an over-long name is refused\",\n                throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"a\".repeat(Names.MAX_LENGTH + 1))),\n                \"oversized name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a slash is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"../etc/passwd\")),\n                \"path-like name accepted\");\n        ok(\"a doubled dot is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"a..b\")),\n                \"double dot accepted\");\n        ok(\"a reserved name is refused\", throwsIae(() -> Names.normalize(\"admin-portal\")),\n                \"reserved name accepted\");\n        ok(\"an ordinary name is kept\", \"carol_2\".equals(Names.normalize(\"carol_2\")),\n                Names.normalize(\"carol_2\"));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- storage\n\n    private static void storageRoundTrip() throws Exception {\n        Path file = scratch.resolve(\"store-roundtrip.tsv\");\n        CredentialStore first = new CredentialStore(file);\n        first.put(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray(), Roles.SUPERVISOR);\n        first.put(\"bob\", \"Bob-Passw0rd-22\".toCharArray(), Roles.OPERATOR);\n\n        String onDisk = Files.readString(file, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        ok(\"no plaintext password is written\",\n                !onDisk.contains(\"Alice-Passw0rd-1\") && !onDisk.contains(\"Bob-Passw0rd-22\"),\n                \"plaintext found in the store\");\n        ok(\"each stored row has exactly five fields\", onDisk.lines()\n                .filter(line -> !line.startsWith(\"#\"))\n                .allMatch(line -> line.chars().filter(c -> c == TAB).count() == 4),\n                quoted(onDisk));\n        ok(\"the credential file is owner read-write only\", ownerOnly(file),\n                \"permissions \" + Files.getPosixFilePermissions(file));\n\n        CredentialStore reopened = new CredentialStore(file);\n        ok(\"both rows survive a reload\", reopened.size() == 2, \"size \" + reopened.size());\n        ok(\"a reloaded row keeps the stored salt\",\n                reopened.find(\"alice\").salt().length == Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES, \"wrong salt length\");\n        ok(\"removing an account persists\",\n                reopened.remove(\"bob\") && new CredentialStore(file).size() == 1,\n                \"removal not persisted\");\n\n        Path broken = scratch.resolve(\"store-broken.tsv\");\n        Files.writeString(broken, \"# comment\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"goodrow\\t100000\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64() + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"shortsalt\\t100000\\t\" + shortSaltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64() + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"badrole\\t100000\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64() + \"\\twizard\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"junkrow\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"tinyiter\\t99\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64() + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"shortkey\\t100000\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + shortKeyB64() + \"\\toperator\" + NEWLINE,\n                StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore loaded = new CredentialStore(broken);\n        ok(\"one good row is loaded from a messy file\", loaded.size() == 1, \"size \" + loaded.size());\n        ok(\"the five bad rows are counted as unreadable\", loaded.malformedRows() == 5,\n                \"malformedRows \" + loaded.malformedRows());\n\n        Path notAFile = scratch.resolve(\"store-is-a-directory\");\n        Files.createDirectories(notAFile);\n        boolean threw = false;\n        try {\n            new CredentialStore(notAFile);\n        } catch (IOException expected) {\n            threw = true;\n        }\n        ok(\"a directory where the store belongs is an error\", threw, \"directory accepted as store\");\n\n        Path tamperedPath = scratch.resolve(\"store-tampered.tsv\");\n        AuditLog tamperAudit = new AuditLog(scratch.resolve(\"store-tampered-audit.log\"));\n        CredentialStore live = new CredentialStore(tamperedPath);\n        live.put(\"carol\", \"Carol-Passw0rd-3\".toCharArray(), Roles.SUPERVISOR);\n        CredentialStore.AdminCredential before = live.find(\"carol\");\n        Files.writeString(tamperedPath, \"carol\\t100000\\t\" + saltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64()\n                + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore tampered = new CredentialStore(tamperedPath);\n        LoginService tamperService = new LoginService(tampered, tamperAudit, 40,\n                LoginService.DEFAULT_LOCKOUT_MILLIS, LoginService.DEFAULT_SESSION_TTL_MILLIS);\n        ok(\"the real salt of the original row was 16 bytes\",\n                before.salt().length == Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES, \"fixture salt already wrong\");\n        ok(\"the replaced row really differs from the original\",\n                !Arrays.equals(before.key(), tampered.find(\"carol\").key()), \"key unchanged\");\n        ok(\"the genuine password stops working once the key is swapped\",\n                tamperService.verify(\"carol\", \"Carol-Passw0rd-3\".toCharArray())\n                        .status() == LoginService.Status.DENIED,\n                \"swapped key still logged in\");\n    }\n\n    // ----------------------------------------------------------- authenticate\n\n    private static void authentication() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"auth\", 40);\n        char[] callerArray = \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray();\n        LoginService.AuthResult good = f.service.verify(\"alice\", callerArray);\n        ok(\"the right password is granted\", good.status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                String.valueOf(good.status()));\n        ok(\"the granted result carries the stored role\", Roles.SUPERVISOR.equals(good.role()),\n                String.valueOf(good.role()));\n        ok(\"the password array of the caller is wiped after a grant\", isAllNul(callerArray),\n                \"characters left in the caller array\");\n\n        char[] wrongArray = \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray();\n        LoginService.AuthResult wrong = f.service.verify(\"alice\", wrongArray);\n        ok(\"the wrong password is denied\", wrong.status() == LoginService.Status.DENIED,\n                String.valueOf(wrong.status()));\n        ok(\"a denial gives away nothing\", \"the username or password is not correct\"\n                .equals(wrong.message()), wrong.message());\n        ok(\"the password array of the caller is wiped after a denial\", isAllNul(wrongArray),\n                \"characters left in the caller array\");\n\n        ok(\"the username is matched case-insensitively\", f.service.verify(\"ALICE\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray()).status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                \"mixed case refused\");\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult ghost = f.service.verify(\"nobody\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"an unknown username is denied\", ghost.status() == LoginService.Status.DENIED,\n                String.valueOf(ghost.status()));\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult blank = f.service.verify(\"alice\", new char[0]);\n        ok(\"an empty password is refused cheaply, without the derivation\",\n                blank.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED && blank.elapsedNanos() < 50_000_000L,\n                blank.status() + \" in \" + ms(blank.elapsedNanos()) + \" ms\");\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult nul = f.service.verify(\"alice\", (\"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NUL).toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password plus a trailing null is refused\",\n                nul.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, String.valueOf(nul.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult tabbed = f.service.verify(\"alice\" + TAB,\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password for a tab-padded name is refused\",\n                tabbed.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, String.valueOf(tabbed.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult surrogate = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                (\"Alice-Passw0rd-\" + (char) 0xD842).toCharArray());\n        ok(\"an unpaired surrogate password is refused\",\n                surrogate.status() == LoginService.Status.REJECTED, String.valueOf(surrogate.status()));\n\n        Path storePath = f.store.path();\n        CredentialStore.AdminCredential aliceRow = f.store.find(\"alice\");\n        Files.writeString(storePath, \"alice\\t100000\\t\" + shortSaltB64() + \"\\t\" + keyB64()\n                + \"\\tsupervisor\" + NEWLINE, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        CredentialStore reloaded = new CredentialStore(storePath);\n        ok(\"a row with a short salt is never loaded\", reloaded.size() == 0, \"size \" + reloaded.size());\n        ok(\"the rewritten row was really the fixture row\",\n                aliceRow.salt().length == Pbkdf2.SALT_BYTES && reloaded.find(\"alice\") == null,\n                \"fixture row not replaced as intended\");\n    }\n\n    private static boolean isAllNul(char[] value) {\n        for (char c : value) {\n            if (c != '\\0') {\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n        return true;\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------- lockout per name\n\n    private static void lockoutIsPerAccount() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"lockout\", 40);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        }\n        ok(\"three failures leave the account usable\", f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray()).status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                \"locked too early\");\n\n        LoginService.AuthResult third = null;\n        for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) {\n            third = f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        }\n        ok(\"the attempt that reaches the limit says locked\",\n                third != null && third.status() == LoginService.Status.LOCKED,\n                String.valueOf(third == null ? null : third.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult whileLocked = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password is refused while locked\",\n                whileLocked.status() == LoginService.Status.LOCKED,\n                String.valueOf(whileLocked.status()));\n        LoginService.AuthResult otherName = f.service.verify(\"bob\", \"Bob-Passw0rd-22\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"another administrator is not locked out\",\n                otherName.status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED, String.valueOf(otherName.status()));\n        ok(\"the failure count for the other name is still zero\",\n                f.service.failuresFor(\"bob\") == 0, \"count \" + f.service.failuresFor(\"bob\"));\n        f.service.clearLock(\"alice\");\n        ok(\"a supervisor unlock restores access\", f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray()).status() == LoginService.Status.GRANTED,\n                \"unlock did not help\");\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------- equal cost\n\n    private static void equalCostBranches() throws Exception {\n        Fixture plain = fixture(\"cost\", 400);\n        Fixture locked = fixture(\"cost-lock\", 2, 24L * 60L * 60L * 1000L);\n        for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {\n            locked.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        }\n        ok(\"the timing fixture account is locked before the run\",\n                locked.service.isLockedOut(\"alice\"), \"not locked\");\n\n        List<Long> knownBad = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> unknown = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> whileLocked = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> badName = new ArrayList<>();\n        List<Long> unusablePassword = new ArrayList<>();\n        for (int round = 0; round < 3; round++) {\n            knownBad.add(plain.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            unknown.add(plain.service.verify(\"nobody-here\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            whileLocked.add(locked.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            badName.add(plain.service.verify(\"x\" + TAB, \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n            unusablePassword.add(plain.service.verify(\"alice\", (\"p\" + NUL).toCharArray())\n                    .elapsedNanos());\n        }\n        long realWork = median(knownBad);\n        ok(\"a real derivation is timed, not a rounding artefact\", realWork > 20_000_000L,\n                \"median \" + ms(realWork) + \" ms\");\n        ok(\"an unknown username costs the same as a known one\",\n                withinFactor(unknown, knownBad, 2.0), ratio(unknown, knownBad) + \" (\"\n                        + ms(median(unknown)) + \" against \" + ms(realWork) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"an attempt while locked costs the same\",\n                withinFactor(whileLocked, knownBad, 2.0), ratio(whileLocked, knownBad) + \" (\"\n                        + ms(median(whileLocked)) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"a malformed username costs the same as a real check\",\n                withinFactor(badName, knownBad, 2.0), ratio(badName, knownBad) + \" (\"\n                        + ms(median(badName)) + \" ms)\");\n        ok(\"the four timed branches stay inside one band\",\n                spread(knownBad, unknown, whileLocked, badName) <= 2.0,\n                \"spread \" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, \"%.2f\",\n                        spread(knownBad, unknown, whileLocked, badName)) + \" times\");\n        ok(\"an unusable password is refused at least ten times cheaper\",\n                median(unusablePassword) < realWork / 10, ms(median(unusablePassword))\n                        + \" ms against \" + ms(realWork) + \" ms\");\n    }\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------- audit trail\n\n    private static void auditTrail() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"audit\", 40);\n        f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        f.service.verify(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        f.service.verify(\"nobody\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-9\".toCharArray());\n        List<String> lines = f.audit.tail(100);\n        ok(\"every attempt is written down\", lines.size() == 3, \"records \" + lines.size());\n        ok(\"grants and denials are labelled\", lines.get(0).contains(\"AUTH_GRANTED\")\n                && lines.get(1).contains(\"AUTH_DENIED\") && lines.get(2).contains(\"AUTH_DENIED\"),\n                String.join(\" | \", lines));\n        ok(\"records are numbered in order\", lines.get(0).startsWith(\"1\" + TAB)\n                && lines.get(2).startsWith(\"3\" + TAB), \"numbering \" + lines.get(2));\n        ok(\"no password reaches the log\",\n                !Files.readString(f.audit.path()).contains(\"Alice-Passw0rd\"),\n                \"password found in the log\");\n        ok(\"a reopened log continues the numbering\",\n                new AuditLog(f.audit.path()).tail(100).get(2).startsWith(\"3\" + TAB),\n                \"numbering reset on reopen\");\n    }\n\n    private static void auditTrailCannotBeForged() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"audit-sanitise\", 40);\n        f.audit.record(\"EVA\", \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"9999\" + TAB + \"FORGED\", \"detail\");\n        List<String> lines = f.audit.tail(10);\n        ok(\"hostile input stays inside one record\", lines.size() == 1, \"records \" + lines.size());\n        ok(\"newlines and tabs are replaced\", lines.get(0).contains(\"alice?9999?FORGED\"),\n                lines.get(0));\n        ok(\"a record keeps exactly five fields\",\n                lines.get(0).chars().filter(c -> c == TAB).count() == 4, \"field count off\");\n        f.audit.record(\"EVB\", \"x\".repeat(300), \"d\");\n        List<String> after = f.audit.tail(10);\n        ok(\"an over-long field is truncated\",\n                after.get(1).split(\"\\t\", -1)[3].length() == AuditLog.MAX_FIELD_CHARS,\n                \"field not truncated\");\n        ok(\"an empty field is written as a dash\", \"-\".equals(AuditLog.clean(\"\")),\n                AuditLog.clean(\"\"));\n        ok(\"the sanitiser is applied to the event name too\", AuditLog.clean(\"A\" + NEWLINE + \"B\")\n                .equals(\"A?B\"), AuditLog.clean(\"A\" + NEWLINE + \"B\"));\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------------- fail closed\n\n    private static void failClosedWhenUnrecordable() throws Exception {\n        Path control = scratch.resolve(\"audit-control.log\");\n        AuditLog good = new AuditLog(control);\n        good.record(\"CTRL\", \"alice\", \"one\");\n        good.record(\"CTRL\", \"alice\", \"two\");\n        ok(\"the control log really accepts records\",\n                Files.readString(control).lines().count() == 2, CredentialStore(scratch.resolve(\"failclosed.tsv\"));\n        store.put(\"alice\", \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray(), Roles.SUPERVISOR);\n        Fixture f = new Fixture(store, new AuditLog(blocked));\n        LoginService.AuthResult refused = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        ok(\"the right password is refused when the log cannot be written\",\n                refused.status() == LoginService.Status.RECORDING_FAILED,\n                String.valueOf(refused.status()));\n        ok(\"the refusal explains itself\", String.valueOf(refused.role()));\n        ok(\"a session without a role cannot run admin commands\",\n                !f.service.authorized(f.service.issueSession(\"alice\", null)),\n                \"a role-less session was accepted\");\n\n        String transcript = runPortal(f, \"alice\" + NEWLINE + \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\" + NEWLINE\n                + \"whoami\" + NEWLINE);\n        ok(\"the command line portal also grants nothing without a log\",\n                transcript.contains(\"access denied.\") && !transcript.contains(\"signed in as alice\"),\n                quoted(transcript));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------- sessions\n\n    private static void sessionLifecycle() throws Exception {\n        Fixture f = fixture(\"session\", 40);\n        LoginService.AuthResult granted = f.service.verify(\"alice\",\n                \"Alice-Passw0rd-1\".toCharArray());\n        LoginService.Session live = f.service.issueSession(granted.username(), granted.role());\n        ok(\"a fresh session authorises admin commands\", f.service.authorized(live),\n                \"fresh session refused\");\n        LoginService.Session expired = new LoginService.Session(live.token(), live.username(),\n                live.role(), System.currentTimeMillis() - 1);\n        ok(\"an expired session does not\", !f.service.authorized(expired), \"expired session kept\");\n        ok(\"a session with no token does not\",\n                !f.service.authorized(new LoginService.Session(null, \"alice\", Roles.SUPERVISOR,\n                        System.currentTimeMillis() + 60_000)), \"tokenless session kept\");\n        ok(\"a session with an unknown role does not\",\n                !f.service.authorized(new LoginService.Session(live.token(), \"alice\", \"wizard\",\n                        System.currentTimeMillis() + 60_000)), \"unknown role kept\");\n        LoginService.Session second = f.service.issueSession(granted.username(), granted.role());\n        ok(\"sessions get a different token each time\", !live.token().equals(second.token()),\n                \"token reused\");\n        ok(\"only a supervisor may manage accounts\",\n                Roles.mayManageAccounts(Roles.SUPERVISOR) && !Roles.mayManageAccounts(Roles.OPERATOR),\n                \"role table wrong\");\n        ok(\"an auditor may still read the trail\", Roles.mayReadAudit(Roles.AUDITOR),\n                \"auditor refused\");\n    }\n\n    // -------------------------------------------------------- child process CLI\n\n    private static void commandLine() throws Exception {\n        Path home = scratch.resolve(\"cli-bootstrap\");\n        String out = runCli(home, line(\"alice\", \"weak\", CLI_PASS, CLI_PASS, \"alice\", CLI_PASS,\n                \"whoami\", \"quit\"), null);\n        ok(\"the child creates the first supervisor\",\n                out.contains(\"created alice with role supervisor\"), tail(out));\n        ok(\"a weak password asks to be repeated once\",\n                count(out, \"new password: \") == 2 && problem\", out.contains(\"rejected: the password must be\"),\n                tail(out));\n        ok(\"the child signs in and answers whoami\",\n                out.contains(\"signed in as alice\")\n                        && out.contains(\"you are alice with role supervisor\"), tail(out));\n        ok(\"the child closes when asked\", out.contains(\"the portal is closing.\"), tail(out));\n\n        String afterQuit = runCli(home, line(\"alice\", CLI_PASS, \"admins\", \"quit\"), null);\n        ok(\"the account written by the first child survived\",\n                afterQuit.contains(\"alice  supervisor\"), tail(afterQuit));\n\n        Path second = scratch.resolve(\"cli-roles\");\n        runCli(second, line(\"alice\", CLI_PASS, CLI_PASS, \"alice\", CLI_PASS, \"create carol\",\n                CLI_CAROL_PASS, CLI_CAROL_PASS, \"quit\"), null);\n        String asOperator = runCli(second, line(\"carol\", CLI_CAROL_PASS, \"create dave\", \"audit 5\",\n                \"quit\"), null);\n        ok(\"an operator cannot create accounts\",\n                asOperator.contains(\"refused: only a supervisor may do that.\")\n                        && !asOperator.contains(\"created dave\"), + \" against \" + quoted(unknownUser));\n        ok(\"the denial text is the generic one\",\n                \"the username or password is not correct\".equals(wrongPassword), quoted(wrongPassword));\n\n        Path dead = scratch.resolve(\"cli-nolog\");\n        runCli(dead, line(\"alice\", CLI_PASS, CLI_PASS), null);\n        Files.createDirectories(dead.resolve(\"audit.log\"));\n        String noLog = runCli(dead, line(\"alice\", CLI_PASS, \"whoami\"), null);\n        ok(\"without a usable log the child grants nothing\",\n                noLog.contains(\"access denied.\") && !noLog.contains(\"signed in as alice\"),\n                tail(noLog));\n        ok(\"and it says the login could not be recorded\",\n                noLog.contains(\"access refused: the login could not be recorded.\"), tail(noLog));\n\n        Path aborted = runCli(scratch.resolve(\"cli-empty-abort\"), line(\"al\", \"\"), null);\n        ok(\"an unusable bootstrap keeps the portal locked\",\n                aborted.contains(\"the portal stays locked.\"), tail(aborted));\n\n        Path self = scratch.resolve(\"cli-selftest\");\n        String report = runCli(self, line(\"alice\", CLI_PASS, CLI_PASS), \"self-test\");\n        ok(\"self-test refuses all three probes\",\n                report.contains(\"wrong password   -> DENIED\")\n                        && report.contains(\"unknown username -> DENIED\")\n                        && report.contains(\"blank password   -> REJECTED\"), tail(report));\n        ok(\"self-test prints a measurable cost per probe\",\n                report.contains(\"wrong password   -> DENIED in \") && report.contains(\" ms\"),\n                tail(report));\n    }\n\n    private static String line(String... parts) {\n        return String.join(NEWLINE + \"\", parts) + NEWLINE;\n    }\n\n    /** Runs the portal in a child JVM with piped standard input, returning its output. */\n    private static String runCli(Path home, String stdin, String... args) throws Exception {\n        List<String> command = new ArrayList<>(List.of(\n                System.getProperty(\"java.home\") + File.separator + \"bin\" + File.separator + \"java\",\n                \"-cp\", new File(System.getProperty(\"java.class.path\")).getAbsolutePath(),\n                \"-Dportal.home=\" + home.toAbsolutePath(), \"portal.AdminPortal\"));\n        command.addAll(Arrays.asList(args));\n        ProcessBuilder builder = new ProcessBuilder(command);\n        builder.redirectErrorStream(true);\n        Process child = builder.start();\n        try (OutputStream stdinStream = child.getOutputStream()) {\n            stdinStream.write(stdin.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));\n            stdinStream.flush();\n        }\n        String output;\n        try (InputStream in = child.getInputStream()) {\n            output = new String(in.readAllBytes(), StandardCharsets.UTF_8);\n        }\n        int code = child.waitFor();\n        if (code != 0 && code != 2) {\n            fail(\"the portal child exited with \" + code + \": \" + tail(output));\n        }\n        return output;\n    }\n\n    /** Runs an in-process portal with piped input, returning its whole output. */\n    private static String It keeps its\naccounts in a file, remembers nothing in plain text, and writes down every login\nattempt.\n\nWritten for Java 17. No libraries, no build tool, nothing to download: only the\nJava Development Kit.\n\n## What it does\n\n1. It asks for a username, then a password (hidden while you type it).\n2. It checks the password against a stored, salted hash.\n3. If the check passes, you get a session that lasts 15 minutes and can run the\n   administrator commands. If it fails, you are told only that the username or\n   password is not correct.\n4. Every attempt, successful or not, is appended to an audit log.\n\nThe first time you run it there are no accounts at all, so it asks you to create\nthe first supervisor before it will accept any login.\n\n## How to build it, run it and check it\n\n```sh\n# compile\njavac -Xlint:all -Werror -d out $(find src -name '*.java')\n\n# run it (accounts and log go in ~/.admin-portal by default)\njava -cp out portal.AdminPortal\n\n# choose somewhere else for its files\njava -cp out -Dportal.home=/var/lib/admin-portal portal.AdminPortal\n\n# run the checks (compiles again, then reports one line per check)\n./run-checks.sh\n```\n\n`java -cp out portal.AdminPortal self-test` is also available. It creates\nnothing new; it just tries three wrong logins and prints how long each took, so\nyou can see the timing behaviour described below with your own eyes.\n\n## Files\n\n| Path | What it is |\n| --- | --- |\n| `src/portal/AdminPortal.java` | The command line program: prompts, commands, sessions. |\n| `src/portal/LoginService.java` | Verifies a username and password, counts failures, hands out sessions. |\n| `src/portal/Pbkdf2.java` | Turns a password into a hash, and does the same amount of work for refusals. |\n| `src/portal/CredentialStore.java` | Reads and writes the account file atomically. |\n| `src/portal/PasswordPolicy.java` | Rules a new password has to satisfy. |\n| `src/portal/Names.java` | Rules for usernames. |\n| `src/portal/AuditLog.java` | The append-only record of what happened. |\n| `src/portal/Roles.java` | Which role is allowed to do what. |\n| `src/portal/PortalChecks.java` | The checks. Not needed to run the portal. |\n| `run-checks.sh` | Compiles everything and runs the checks. |\n\nIn the directory the portal uses (its \"home\") it keeps two files:\n\n- `credentials.tsv` — one line per account: username, iteration count, salt,\n  hash, role. The password itself is never in this file. The file is created so\n  that only its owner can read or write it.\n- `audit.log` — one line per event: a number, the time, the event, who it was\n  about, and a short note.\n\n## Commands\n\nOnce you are signed in (`help` lists these too). Everything except `help` needs\na live session.\n\n| Command | Who may use it | What it does |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| `whoami` | anyone signed in | name and role, and when the session ends |\n| `status` | anyone signed in | how many accounts there are and where the files are |\n| `audit [n]` | anyone signed in | the last `n` log records, 20 if you leave `n` out |\n| `admins` | supervisor | list accounts, their roles, and who is locked |\n| `create [name]` | supervisor | add an operator and set their password |\n| `reset [name]` | supervisor | set somebody's new password and clear their lock |\n| `remove [name]` | supervisor | delete an account |\n| `unlock [name]` | supervisor | clear an account's lock early |\n| `logout` | anyone signed in | drop the session, stay connected |\n| `quit` | anyone signed in | close the portal (`exit` works too) |\n\n`remove` refuses to delete the last remaining account, because that would lock\neveryone out of their own portal.\n\n## How it protects the password and the login\n\n**Passwords are stored as salted hashes.** Each account gets its own random\n16-byte salt and its password is stretched with PBKDF2-HMAC-SHA256, 100,000\nrounds, into a 32-byte key. A stolen account file therefore gives an attacker\nnothing they can use directly, and one slow hash per guess makes guessing\nexpensive. When a password is checked the array holding it is overwritten\nimmediately afterwards, whether the check succeeded or not.\n\n**A wrong answer tells you nothing useful.** Whether the username does not\nexist, the account is locked, or the password is simply wrong, the message is the\nsame: *\"the username or password is not correct\"*.\n\n**Answering time is the same in all three cases.** Checking a password takes\nabout 150 milliseconds here, and that cost would be a giveaway: an unknown\nusername could be answered for free, so anyone could sweep through a list of names\nand see which ones have accounts, without ever guessing a password. So the\nbranches that cannot possibly succeed spend the same 150 milliseconds on a\nlook-alike hash that is never compared against anything. A refusal that is\ndeliberately cheap is the *unusable input* one (an empty password, a password\nwith a null character in it), which is answered in well under a millisecond —\nthat is safe because those refusals do not depend on the username at all.\n\n**Failures are counted per account, not globally.** Five wrong tries lock that\none username for 15 minutes; a supervisor can clear the lock early with\n`unlock`. A successful sign-in clears the count. Another administrator keeps\nworking normally while somebody else is locked out.\n\n**Passwords have to be worth storing.** At least 10 characters, no more than\n1024, containing a letter and a digit and drawing on at least three kinds of\ncharacter (upper case, lower case, digits, other), and it must not contain the\nusername. New passwords are typed twice; they are checked *before* you are asked\nto type them a second time, so a rejected password does not swallow the line you\nmeant for the next question.\n\n**Two odd inputs are refused rather than stored, because they would let two\ndifferent passwords unlock the same account:** a password containing a null\ncharacter (the hashing API quietly drops a trailing one) and one with an\nincomplete character in it (the API quietly replaces it with a question mark).\n\n**Usernames are matched ignoring case, and padding is refused, not trimmed.**\n`Alice` and `alice` are the same account, on purpose. But `alice` followed by a\ntab or a newline is *not* `alice`: trimming would quietly turn a mistyped or\ncrafted name into a valid login. Usernames are limited to `a-z`, `0-9`, `.`, `_`\nand `-`, which also keeps them safe to put in the account file and the log.\n\n**Nothing is granted unless it was written down.** A successful login is\nrecorded in the audit log *before* access is given. If the log cannot be written\n— the disk is full, the path is broken — the login is refused with *\"access\nrefused: the login could not be recorded\"*. A portal that cannot tell you who\ncame in is not one worth opening. The same rule applies to `create`, `reset`,\n`remove` and `unlock`.\n\n**The log cannot be forged by its own input.** Text that arrives from user input\nis written with any newline or tab replaced by `?` and any over-long field cut\nshort, so nobody can end a record early and append a fake one, or use the log to\nspeak terminal control codes at whoever reads it.\n\n**A damaged account file is skipped, not trusted.** A row whose salt or hash has\nthe wrong length, whose iteration count is absurd, or whose role is unknown is\ncounted and ignored; the portal starts with the remaining rows and `status`\nreports how many were unreadable. The file is rewritten through a temporary file\nand moved into place, so a crash mid-write cannot leave a half-written account\nfile.\n\n## What was actually verified\n\n`./run-checks.sh` runs 111 checks and finishes in about 15 seconds. All 111 pass.\nRoughly half drive the classes directly; the other half start the real program as\na separate process with piped input, because answering questions in memory does\nnot reproduce how a terminal consumes lines one at a time.\n\nThe checks cover, among others: the right password is accepted and a wrong one\nis not; the wrong password and an unknown username produce the *same* sentence\nfrom the real command line program; a padded username is refused instead of\ntrimmed; a null character in a password cannot smuggle a login through; the file\ncontains no plaintext and can only be read by its owner; a genuine account stops\nworking if an attacker swaps its stored hash; the failure count locks one account\nand leaves the others alone; hostile text cannot forge a second log record; and\nwhen the log cannot be written, nothing is granted.\n\nThe timing claims are checked by measuring each branch three times and insisting\nthat all four branches land within a factor of two of each other, while an\nunusable password must come back at least ten times faster. Measured once on this\nmachine by `self-test`: wrong password 149.9 ms, unknown username 149.7 ms,\nunusable password 0.2 ms.\n\nChecks that pass by accident are the real risk, so 10 deliberate defects were\nintroduced one at a time — remove the equal-cost work from each refusal branch,\nlock every account when one fails, grant access even when the log write failed,\naccept a null character in a password, trim usernames instead of refusing them,\nwrite log fields unfiltered, compare keys with a shortcutting comparison, let\nevery role manage accounts — and the checks noticed every one (10 caught, 0\nmissed, no compile failures, no runner errors). Between one and five checks\nfailed per defect, and the failure named the broken behaviour.\n\nTwo of those deserve a note. The shortcutting key comparison changes no\nobservable behaviour at all, so the only honest way to pin it is a check that\nreads the source text of that one method and insists it uses the constant-time\ncomparison — with a companion check that the method was found in the first place,\nso the guard cannot pass on an empty capture. And when the log-filtering defect\nwas inserted, the suite caught it but then stopped early with an\narray-out-of-bounds error inside the checks themselves: the forged record had\nfewer fields than the next check expected. It was still a clear failure, only a\nnoisier one than it should be.\n\nOne limitation is stated plainly rather than tested away: `create` always makes an\n`operator` and the bootstrap makes a `supervisor`, so the third role, `auditor`,\ncan only appear if the account file is edited by hand. Its permissions are\ndefined and checked, but no command can produce it.\n\n## Known limits\n\n- **One process at a time.** The account file is read at start-up and rewritten\n  whole. Two portals sharing one home directory will overwrite each other's\n  changes. There is no file locking.\n- **Locks live in memory.** Restarting the portal clears every lock, deliberately\n  simple, but it also means a restart is an escape hatch.\n- **The account file must be protected by the operating system.** Everything here\n  assumes nobody else can read it; run the portal as a dedicated user and keep\n  the directory owner-only.\n- **The audit log is append-only by convention.** It is not signed or chained, so\n  someone who can write that file can add records (they cannot use user input to\n  do it, and they cannot make a record look like two). Rotate and ship it\n  elsewhere if you need real evidence.\n- **No limit on username guessing across accounts.** Locks are per username, so\n  spreading attempts over many names is not slowed down. Put a rate limit in\n  front of it if it is ever reachable over a network.\n- **Piped input is not private.** When standard input is not a terminal, passwords\n  arrive as plain lines from whatever is feeding the pipe. On a real terminal the\n  password is hidden by the console.\n- **Timing results are statistical.** The equal-cost checks use ratios on a\n  loaded machine; they are sound as a regression guard, not as a claim about your\n  hardware.\n\n## Passwords used by the checks\n\nThe checks create throwaway accounts in a temporary directory and delete them\nagain. Those passwords (`Alice-Passw0rd-1`, `Bob-Passw0rd-22` and the ones in\n`PortalChecks.java`) are visible in this repository and exist only to make the\nchecks reproducible. So a fail-closed test that does `Files.delete(log)` and then\nexpects the next append to throw instead gets a **fresh empty log and a\nsuccessful write** - the guard under test never fails, and any assertion built on\n\"the trail is broken\" passes for the wrong reason.\n\n**What actually forces the failure (verified 2026-09-12, OpenJDK 17, running as\nroot):** point the log path at a **directory** -\n`Files.createDirectories(logPath)` then `append(...)` throws\n`FileAlreadyExistsException` (an `IOException`) every time. A permission bit is\n*not* an option here: `file.setWritable(false)` is ignored by root, and this box\nruns as root.\n\n**How to apply:** to test any \"recording failed, so refuse\" path, replace the log\npath with a directory of the same name. Assert the denial *and* that no session\nexists. Also confirm the control case first - write a few records and assert they\nare on disk - otherwise a broken fixture makes the fail-closed test pass\nvacuously.\n\n**The substitution is not idempotent (re-verified 2026-09-13, OpenJDK 17):**\n`Files.createDirectories(p)` on a path that already holds a **regular file** throws\n`FileAlreadyExistsException` - it only tolerates an existing *directory*. A fixture\nthat blocks a path its own setup just wrote (store file, audit log) therefore dies\nwith that exception and the whole suite reports \"checks threw\". Write one helper -\n`if (Files.isRegularFile(p)) Files.delete(p); Files.createDirectories(p);` - and use\nit for every blocked-path case instead of calling `createDirectories` inline.\n\nRelated trap in the same constructor: reading the last sequence number from an\nexisting-but-unreadable log must not be \"repaired\" by starting at 1, because the\nportal would then run with a working gate and a corrupt trail. Swallowing that\nread and letting every later append fail closed is the stricter outcome.\n"}]}},{"type":"assistant","uuid":"1226a19b-85fc-4435-b49f-24a78d311ec8","session_id":"48fb435d-3dcb-4172-b33a-70e76424be32","parent_tool_use_id":null,"message":{"id":"1226a19b-85fc-4435-b49f-24a78d311ec8","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"vllm-general","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The existing memory already covers the directory-substitution idempotency trap thoroughly, including the helper. That second half converted the same\nmutant from \"suite died\" into `the 'malformed input' checks ran to completion\n[ArrayIndexOutOfBoundsException]`, an attributable kill. Verify a detached run is alive by walking `/proc/*/cmdline`\n(this box has no `ps`/`pgrep`), and confirm only **one** instance is running before trusting timing\nchecks — two concurrent batteries under PBKDF2 destroy the timing suite.\n\n**Before accepting a survivor, ask whether it breaks a *documented* contract you simply never\nasserted.** Measured 2026-09-12 on a 46-mutant battery (final: 45 killed, 1 documented survivor).\nTwo survivors looked like noise and were real holes: `account-tostring-leaks-hash` (the suite\nchecked the *request's* `toString` redaction but never the *stored record's*) and\n`caller-buffer-clobbered` (the suite checked \"the caller's buffer still holds the password after\nlogin\", which stays true when the object adopts the caller's array -- adoption only shows up once\nsomething *writes* through the shared array). Both killed by asserting the contract that was already\nclaimed: redaction on every type that holds a secret, and array *identity* (`retained != given`)\nplus \"erasing the object does not erase the caller's buffer\".\n\n**Do not force a kill with a false contract.** The same session produced two invalid attempts to\nkill a wipe-the-local-copy mutant: wiping a test-owned array and asserting it was wiped\n(self-satisfying), and asserting a login call erased the *request's retained* array when it only\nerases its own working copy (fails on correct code, so it \"kills\" every mutant by being always\nred). If the only way to observe a mutant is to weaken a real defensive choice (un-`final` a class\nso a fake can capture an array), that mutant is equivalent: report it, do not restructure the design\nfor the score.\n\n**Fix a crash-kill into an assertion-kill; do not just label it.** Measured 2026-09-12 on a\n22-mutant sign-in battery. The `email = ?` -> `email LIKE ?` mutant \"killed\" only because the\nwildcard fixture (`%`) made the real engine throw inside the lookup and the uncaught `SQLException`\nended the run -- the battery printed the stack trace where the failing-check name should be, so the\nkill was not attributable to any assertion. Wrapping the lookup so it returns `\"row\" / \"no row\" /\n\"error: msg\"` and asserting `\"no row\"` turned the same mutant into a named failure\n(`the lookup for a % address finds no account`). Have the harness print a distinct reason when a\nsuite dies without asserting (`suite died instead of asserting`), then treat every such row as a\ntest-quality bug and fix the assertion, not the label.\n\n**\"No secret in the file\" needs a search of the *decoded* fields, not of the file text.**\nMeasured 2026-09-12 on a Java sign-in program (18 mutants). Replacing the KDF digest with\n`Base64(password bytes)` -- trivially reversible, the exact defect the program exists to avoid --\nsurvived a suite that asserted the raw file does not contain the plaintext, its base64, or its hex.\nIt cannot: the field is already base64, so the inner base64 is nested inside another base64 layer\nand no needle of the password appears in the text. Fix: parse the stored record, *decode* the salt\nand digest fields, and `containsBytes(decodedDigest, passwordBytes)` (plus the base64-of-password\nform). That kills the mutant with the assertion that names the property. Generalize: a\n\"nothing recoverable is stored\" test over an encoded container must look inside the encoding.\n\n**A third survivor category: guards that are unreachable by platform contract, not by\ngranularity.** Measured 2026-09-12 on a 41-mutant battery (final: 39 killed, 2 documented).\nDeleting `if (digest.length != HASH_BYTES)` and deleting a constructor self-test that re-derives a\nprobe digest both survived, and no suite can kill them: PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256 at 256 bits always\nreturns exactly 32 bytes, and the probe guard only fires if the KDF were non-deterministic -- the\nconstructor's own `derive()` call already rules that out.
